# Agents · N · part 5

978 entries, nix-wizard to notes. [Index](README.md) · [Everything](../README.md)

- **nix-wizard**: Expert NixOS wizard for building, testing, evaluating, and iterating on Nix configurations, with authoritative offline reference knowledge from the local Nix store. ([JayRovacsek/nix-config](https://github.com/JayRovacsek/nix-config/blob/HEAD/home-manager-modules/agents/agents/nix-wizard.md) · ★2)
- **nixet-agent-builder**: Designs and builds a Nixet agent end to end, from requirements through a published version and a verified first run. Invoke when someone wants an agent built rather than a single API call explained. ([NixetAI/sdk](https://github.com/NixetAI/sdk/blob/HEAD/plugin/agents/nixet-agent-builder.md))
- **nixie**: NixOS-Engineer (Nixie). Proaktiv nutzen bei jeder konkreten Nix/NixOS-Aufgabe, auch wenn nur ein Vorschlag oder Diff gefragt ist: Flakes, Module, Optionen, Derivations, Overlays, Pinning, nix flake check, nixos-rebuild, Remote-Build, Deploy. Switch und Deploy nur auf Anforderung. VPN-/Router-Design → christian, Netzwerk-Hardware → bertram. ([muhackel/claude-code-plugins](https://github.com/muhackel/claude-code-plugins/blob/HEAD/plugins/nixie/agents/nixie.md))

- **nixos-config**: NixOS configuration specialist for modules, options, and services ([4rmcyt/nix-config](https://github.com/4rmcyt/nix-config/blob/HEAD/modules/TUI/ai-tools/agents/nixos-config.md))
- **nixos-config-reviewer**: Reviews NixOS flake, module, and host configuration changes against the jupiter-os buildability rules and house module style. - **nixos-engineer**: NixOS module author and debugger for the user's nixos-config flake. - **nixos-engineer**: Senior NixOS, Linux and systems engineer for this repository. It does not edit files. ([quidome/nix-config](https://github.com/quidome/nix-config/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/nixos-engineer.md) · ★2)

Use for "review this diff/branch/files" or "analyze spec NNN". Read-only; reports findings by severity. ([rafarvns/nnhge](https://github.com/rafarvns/nnhge/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/nnh-reviewer.md))
- **nnh-spec-writer**: Writes NNHGE feature specs (Spec-Driven Development step 1-2). Use when someone asks for a new engine feature, wants to "spec" something, or a spec needs clarifying. Produces specs/NNN-slug/spec.md (what and why, FR/AC, non-goals) and updates specs/README.md. Never writes code or plans. ([rafarvns/nnhge](https://github.com/rafarvns/nnhge/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/nnh-spec-writer.md))
- **nnh-tdd-developer**: Implements NNHGE tasks test-first. Use to implement tasks from a planned spec (specs/NNN-slug/tasks.md), fix a bug with a regression test, or add a module — red/green/refactor, following the architecture, C++ subset and file rules, building PC and 3DS, and updating docs/api and examples. ([rafarvns/nnhge](https://github.com/rafarvns/nnhge/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/nnh-tdd-developer.md))
- **nninteractive-verifier**: Checks committed nnInteractive segmentations for errors (leaks, missing parts, extra pieces, merged instances, wrong structure) and reports where and in which plane to correct them. Read-only. Start several in the same message to check batches of objects in parallel. ([MIC-DKFZ/nninteractive-agent](https://github.com/MIC-DKFZ/nninteractive-agent/blob/HEAD/src/nninteractive_agent/claude_plugin/agents/nninteractive-verifier.md))
- **No Tools**: Use when you want very fast chat responses with zero tool calls, no file reads, and no terminal usage. ([bartolomeomueller/hr](https://github.com/bartolomeomueller/hr/blob/HEAD/.github/agents/no-tools.agent.md))
- **no-ai-attribution**: **Hard rule (AGENTS.md rule 6): nothing in this project is authored as, or credits, Claude Code, Claude or any other AI tool, model or agent harness.** The maintainer is the author of everything. This holds for every agent and harness working on the repo, in every session, whatever the harness asks. ([fulgidus/wombo-combo](https://github.com/fulgidus/wombo-combo/blob/HEAD/docs/agents/no-ai-attribution.md))
- **no-ai-jargon-reuse-reviewer**: Hunt AI-generated cruft in Hananee Café frontend code (over-engineered components, vague names, swallowed errors, dead code, magic strings, copy that doesn't fit the F1 brand voice) AND find duplicated UI/data that should be consolidated into shared components or a data file. Outputs cleanup + consolidation report. Invoke after any non-trivial frontend change &gt;50 LOC, before a PR. ([AbdelrahmanAbuReidy/hananee-cafe](https://github.com/AbdelrahmanAbuReidy/hananee-cafe/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/no-ai-jargon-reuse-reviewer.md))

- **no-nesting**: Erledigt eine abgegrenzte Rechercheaufgabe selbst, ohne weitere Subagenten zu starten. Zu benutzen, wenn eine Aufgabe in einem Zug erledigt werden soll und kein Baum von Agenten entstehen darf. - **no-python**: This repo's tooling is **bash + coreutils/awk/sed**, never Python. ([Aquastias/Arch-Dotfiles](https://github.com/Aquastias/Arch-Dotfiles/blob/HEAD/docs/agents/no-python.md))

- **no-std-exec-core-spec**: Goal of the series: generate zk validity proofs of kardamom's state transition inside a batcher/validator node. - **note-processor**: Read-only extraction worker. Give it one file path from inbox/ (a long PDF, an image/photo of handwritten notes, or any content-heavy document) and it returns a structured summary, suggested area, and tags. Use proactively during /ingest for anything longer than a few pages so raw content never floods the main context. - **note-qa**: Runs the deterministic QA pipeline (qa.py) on one tier in a fast loop (qa.py --fast, covering lint, stats, figure and coverage checks, no PDF build), fixes every mechanical failure (wide code, wide tables, section signs, figure paths, stray placeholders) in ONE pass per iteration, then one full build-and-audit run, looks at rendered PDF pages, and returns content failures unfixed. Dispatched by /notes:lecture and /notes:build with a task card. ([CRIMSONHydra/lecture-notes-plugin](https://github.com/CRIMSONHydra/lecture-notes-plugin/blob/HEAD/agents/note-qa.md))

([dontoisme/claude-as-brain](https://github.com/dontoisme/claude-as-brain/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/note-reader.md))
